Use to answer "what is reflection costing me" for the last day / week, or "is recall satisfying". ([stevengonsalvez/ainb-reflect-memory](https://github.com/stevengonsalvez/ainb-reflect-memory/tree/HEAD/plugin/skills/cost) · ★2)
